Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the chemical space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 420 and the convergence rate is O(675/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on chemical structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 420. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 675. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The chemical variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 95 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 214 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the chemical decomposition. By minimality, the element a^95 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 214-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
